# Bot voices: debate scenes

296 scenes, 3810 lines. Played in /faction by bots of one realm: A opens and the others take sides. `{q}` is an optional opener ("hey", "so"). New writing; the arguments are period knowledge (see RESEARCH-BRIEF.md). Generated by `tools/bot-voices/debates_doc.py`.

## Hibernia (50)

### druid bard warden

Levels 20–50 · 7 lines · about 1 min · roles: B (Druid), C (Bard), D (Warden)

- **A:** druid, bard or warden as the group healer?
- **B:** druid, the heals are biggest and the pet helps in a pinch
- **C:** bard heals and mezzes and gives speed, that is three jobs
- **D:** warden can heal and still take a hit in melee

- **C:** none done perfectly beats one done alone
- **A:** two of the three then. bard always

### hib gen healer alt

Levels 25–50 · 24 lines · about 4 min · roles: B (Druid), C (Bard), D (Warden), E (Leveler/Socialite)

- **A:** "{q} rolling a healer alt. druid, bard or warden?" / "{q} need a hib healer alt, which one would you pick" / "ok healers of hibernia, convince me"
- **B:** "druid. biggest heals in the realm and nobody argues" / "druid, hands down. the heals are why groups survive"
- **C:** "bard. you heal, you run speed, you mez the add. every group wants you" / "bard, because a group without speed is a group walking home slowly"
- **D:** "warden. you can stand in melee and not fold" / "warden, heal from the front line with a weapon in your hand"
- **B:** "a warden heals ok but nobody asks a warden to main heal a hard camp" / "main healing spraggon den as a warden sounds like a long night"
- **D:** "i main healed muire tomb on my warden for a week, nobody died" / "we cleared muire tomb with me as the only healer and it went fine"
- **C:** "muire tomb is not exactly a hard test" / "muire is where everyone learns, it is gentle"
- *(quiet for 25 seconds)*
- **E:** "sorry, which one is the bard, the one with the instrument?" / "wait, which of you plays music"
- **C:** "that is us. songs, speed and a lute on my back" / "yes, the one with the songs. we are hard to miss"
- **E:** "huh, i thought bards only did the speed at the stables" / "i only ever see bards running speed near the stablemaster"
- **B:** "and that is the trap. a bard running speed all fight is not healing" / "the bard is always busy with something, that is my worry"
- **C:** "i change songs when the fight starts, the group lives" / "the speed comes down when the pull lands, it is not hard"
- **D:** *(sometimes)* "half of a warden's job happens before the pull, with the buffs" / "?D: warden buffs make every heal go further, people forget that"
- *(quiet for 30 seconds)*
- **A:** "what about soloing, i log at odd hours" / "solo matters to me too, my friends are never on when i am"
- **B:** "some nature in a druid and the pet does the work" / "druid with a bit of nature solos fine, the pet tanks"
- **D:** "warden solos best of the three. hit it, heal myself, repeat" / "warden easily, i swing and heal myself and keep going"
- **C:** "bard solo is slow, i will give you that one" / "soloing a bard is painful, no argument there"
- **E:** "so warden for solo, druid for groups, bard for everyone's legs" / "sounds like warden alone, druid in groups, bard for speed"
- **B:** "or roll a druid and never solo again, you will be invited everywhere" / "or play a druid and make friends, problem solved"
- *(quiet for 20 seconds)*
- **A:** "leaning druid now honestly" / "ok, druid it is. probably"
